Videos)"></div><span class="B2BZvc"></span><span class="XjoK4b MLAFhd"></span></div></div></section><section class="MHH2Z"><div class="SmeJW"><h2 class="p9xg1"><div>Overview</div></h2></div><div class="p5TRec" jscontroller="Tl2FDf" jsaction="click:i7GaQb(rs1XOd);rcuQ6b:npT2md"><div jsname="ij8cu" class="mN52G oB8Rd"><p>Remove Gemini watermarks from images and videos instantly with AI precision</p><p>Erasio removes the visible sparkle watermark from AI-generated images and videos, directly in your browser.

It does one thing: clean the visible overlay off media you generated yourself, without exporting to a separate editor and without uploading your files anywhere.


WHERE IT WORKS

Erasio adds watermark removal to media you generate on:

• Gemini
• Google AI Studio
• Google Flow
• Google Vids

You can also clean files already saved on your device — drag and drop them into the side panel, paste a screenshot from your clipboard, or pick a file. Supported input: PNG, JPG and WebP images, and MP4 video at 720p or 1080p.


WHAT YOU CAN DO

Clean downloads automatically
When Erasio is on and you download supported media, it removes the watermark before the file is saved.

Preview before you download
By default Erasio shows you the cleaned result next to the original, so you can download it or open the side panel and adjust the removal first. You can switch this off to download immediately.

Fine-tune any result
The side panel gives you a live before/after preview and controls for mask size, position, strength, opacity and edge blending, each with an in-panel explanation of what it does. Save your settings and they are reused next time.

Clean images and video
Images are processed in a moment. Video runs a full local pipeline — read the clip, clean every frame, re-encode — so longer clips take longer and speed depends on your device.

Batch clean (paid plans)
Select several images and videos at once, remove every watermark in one pass, and download them together as a single ZIP.

Insert clean clips in Google Vids
On Google Vids, Erasio can remove the watermark and place the clean clip straight into your project.


HOW IT WORKS

Most cleanup tools inpaint — they delete the watermarked area and generate replacement pixels, so the result is invented detail. Erasio does not. Because the sparkle is a known overlay composited at a known opacity, Erasio reverses that blend mathematically to recover the pixels underneath. On a clean detection the recovered area is the original image data, not a guess.

Results vary with the source. If a mark is faint or sits over busy detail, use the side panel controls to adjust the detection and mask, then re-run.


PROCESSING IS LOCAL

Your images and videos are processed entirely on your device, inside your browser. They are never uploaded to Erasio or to any third-party server, and they are not stored remotely.

Erasio does contact erasio.io for account and credit information only: signing in, checking how many credits you have left, and recording that an export used credits. No media, and no page content, is sent with those requests.


ACCOUNT AND CREDITS

Erasio uses credits. Videos cost more credits than images.

• Without an account you get a small daily allowance to try it.
• A free Erasio account gives you your own daily credits.
• Paid plans provide a larger credit balance and unlock batch multi-select.

Accounts and payments are handled on erasio.io. The extension itself never takes payment; the upgrade button opens the website in a new tab. If you are signed in on erasio.io, the extension picks up that session automatically.


SETTINGS

• Language
• Auto-download results
• Show notifications
• Dark mode
• Automatic in-page removal on or off
• Skip preview and download automatically
• Enable or disable Erasio on Google Flow — useful if Flow&#39;s own watermark toggle is available to you
• Reset to defaults
• Replay the guided tour


LANGUAGES

English, Arabic, German, Spanish, French, Hindi, Italian, Japanese, Korean, Portuguese, Russian, Turkish, Urdu and Simplified Chinese, with right-to-left layout for Arabic and Urdu.


CURRENT LIMITS

• Video input is MP4 at 720p or 1080p.
• Upscaled 1080p and 4K clips from Google Flow are not supported yet.
• Video processing time scales with clip length and your device&#39;s speed.


ON THE WEB

Bulk processing, extra export formats and additional output options are available on the Erasio web app at erasio.io.


RESPONSIBLE USE

Erasio is intended for cleaning media you created and have the rights to use. Please do not use it on other people&#39;s work, or to pass AI-generated media off as something it is not. Erasio only targets the visible sparkle overlay; it is not designed to detect or remove invisible provenance signals such as SynthID.


SUPPORT

Guide: erasio.io/guide
Privacy policy: erasio.io/privacy
Report an issue: erasio.io/contact


Erasio is an independent tool. It is not affiliated with, sponsored by, or endorsed by Google LLC. Gemini, Google AI Studio, Google Flow, Google Vids and Chrome are trademarks of Google LLC.</p></div></div></section><c-wiz jsrenderer="db7dHd" jsshadow jsdata="deferred-i20" Videos)"],null,null,null,null,[[2,"https://www.youtube.com/embed/nm0KCRdT5Lc?rel\u003d0\u0026wmode\u003dopaque\u0026enablejsapi\u003d1"],[1,"https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/TFeMn_sZ4IP0nyOOmBOmIqeLa9F-NW1TcXsg71HdXwSDjRvZFovOnMXYbq0nIibBUT5mVe24R9yTnrmi0OF_qvpr"],[1,"https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/-aipOhqy_YleKpgpGjMegQ9mIfcDXDDlSL5mcLGISSVb7RuJnyECUnAe_jSROHL3GOgEwHc7dkbo-oLiwPfCFwAZdw"],[1,"https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/udLMNWAVTe4qkoKylJ7eqOYUmh9k0yvsjqha21_lknxDzpO395l7-k25qHMxvQUpFw3GR8_rmFKoGORzKlB5oGj_3w"],[1,"https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/rtcGq28M1OY8UWvk1sKvcOoQ1QVAGvz_svcNnEFLnw-Q1VKww7Q8kXOFeDMmduhbjuNOeiRyO9JxJUKkhQY8KxwDKjA"],[1,"https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/48U22i350zOgY_uYRUxiVAQCEjD6sALL_dMrczjuwQ-1P-Ef7srNqiDL_p0EeOxXuXJ5s-U-G5_odUmuQ4qhtcUs_u4"]],"Erasio removes the visible sparkle watermark from AI-generated images and videos, directly in your browser.\n\nIt does one thing: clean the visible overlay off media you generated yourself, without exporting to a separate editor and without uploading your files anywhere.\n\n\nWHERE IT WORKS\n\nErasio adds watermark removal to media you generate on:\n\n• Gemini\n• Google AI Studio\n• Google Flow\n• Google Vids\n\nYou can also clean files already saved on your device — drag and drop them into the side panel, paste a screenshot from your clipboard, or pick a file. Supported input: PNG, JPG and WebP images, and MP4 video at 720p or 1080p.\n\n\nWHAT YOU CAN DO\n\nClean downloads automatically\nWhen Erasio is on and you download supported media, it removes the watermark before the file is saved.\n\nPreview before you download\nBy default Erasio shows you the cleaned result next to the original, so you can download it or open the side panel and adjust the removal first. You can switch this off to download immediately.\n\nFine-tune any result\nThe side panel gives you a live before/after preview and controls for mask size, position, strength, opacity and edge blending, each with an in-panel explanation of what it does. Save your settings and they are reused next time.\n\nClean images and video\nImages are processed in a moment. Video runs a full local pipeline — read the clip, clean every frame, re-encode — so longer clips take longer and speed depends on your device.\n\nBatch clean (paid plans)\nSelect several images and videos at once, remove every watermark in one pass, and download them together as a single ZIP.\n\nInsert clean clips in Google Vids\nOn Google Vids, Erasio can remove the watermark and place the clean clip straight into your project.\n\n\nHOW IT WORKS\n\nMost cleanup tools inpaint — they delete the watermarked area and generate replacement pixels, so the result is invented detail. Erasio does not. Because the sparkle is a known overlay composited at a known opacity, Erasio reverses that blend mathematically to recover the pixels underneath. On a clean detection the recovered area is the original image data, not a guess.\n\nResults vary with the source. If a mark is faint or sits over busy detail, use the side panel controls to adjust the detection and mask, then re-run.\n\n\nPROCESSING IS LOCAL\n\nYour images and videos are processed entirely on your device, inside your browser. They are never uploaded to Erasio or to any third-party server, and they are not stored remotely.\n\nErasio does contact erasio.io for account and credit information only: signing in, checking how many credits you have left, and recording that an export used credits. No media, and no page content, is sent with those requests.\n\n\nACCOUNT AND CREDITS\n\nErasio uses credits. Videos cost more credits than images.\n\n• Without an account you get a small daily allowance to try it.\n• A free Erasio account gives you your own daily credits.\n• Paid plans provide a larger credit balance and unlock batch multi-select.\n\nAccounts and payments are handled on erasio.io. The extension itself never takes payment; the upgrade button opens the website in a new tab. If you are signed in on erasio.io, the extension picks up that session automatically.\n\n\nSETTINGS\n\n• Language\n• Auto-download results\n• Show notifications\n• Dark mode\n• Automatic in-page removal on or off\n• Skip preview and download automatically\n• Enable or disable Erasio on Google Flow — useful if Flow's own watermark toggle is available to you\n• Reset to defaults\n• Replay the guided tour\n\n\nLANGUAGES\n\nEnglish, Arabic, German, Spanish, French, Hindi, Italian, Japanese, Korean, Portuguese, Russian, Turkish, Urdu and Simplified Chinese, with right-to-left layout for Arabic and Urdu.\n\n\nCURRENT LIMITS\n\n• Video input is MP4 at 720p or 1080p.\n• Upscaled 1080p and 4K clips from Google Flow are not supported yet.\n• Video processing time scales with clip length and your device's speed.\n\n\nON THE WEB\n\nBulk processing, extra export formats and additional output options are available on the Erasio web app at erasio.io.\n\n\nRESPONSIBLE USE\n\nErasio is intended for cleaning media you created and have the rights to use. Please do not use it on other people's work, or to pass AI-generated media off as something it is not. Erasio only targets the visible sparkle overlay; it is not designed to detect or remove invisible provenance signals such as SynthID.\n\n\nSUPPORT\n\nGuide: erasio.io/guide\nPrivacy policy: erasio.io/privacy\nReport an issue: erasio.io/contact\n\n\nErasio is an independent tool. It is not affiliated with, sponsored by, or endorsed by Google LLC. Gemini, Google AI Studio, Google Flow, Google Vids and Chrome are trademarks of Google LLC.",[1,3],1,1,["adeel.extension.projects@gmail.com",null,null,null,1,"Adeel Ahmad","Adeel Ahmad","Adeel Ahmad",null,null,"u6330b2158a9b7efdd3b4d7fe2b6d0422"],null,null,"1.1.2",[1787233571,952225000],"547KiB",["Deutsch","English","Türkçe","español","français","italiano","русский","اردو","العربية","हिन्दी","中文（中国）","日本語","한국어"],null,null,null,null,1,[["eccadcnpplfeojakfkjbpicbcndnaekc","https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/_Mi3NZepeFYwSrdp1iGt4FCO9ujLMJstW2HwWfbvqrfSzIbIAno6ZSa9rH4JVa-KUxlqdab4w_MPyIQLudt8ea1RCA","Gemini Watermark Remover - PureGemini",4.133333333333334,30,null,"Remove watermarks from Gemini-generated images and videos.
